Figure 5.
Example of argument generation

Any closed term
t
can be generated with the AB machine, and it is possible to
define the sequence of flags
\mathsf{Seq}(t)
that will be raised. We
write
()
for the empty sequence, and

(F_{1},\ldots,F_{n},(F^{\prime}_{1},\ldots,F^{\prime}_{m}),F_{n+1},\ldots,F_{l})
for the
sequence
(F_{1},\ldots,F_{n},F^{\prime}_{1},\ldots,F^{\prime}_{m},F_{n+1},\ldots,F_{l})
.

{defi}

Given a term
t
, we define
\mathsf{Seq}(t)
as

\displaystyle\mathsf{Seq}(t)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}(\mathsf{SeqTm}(t),\medstar)

\displaystyle\mathsf{SeqTm}(n)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}(\mathsf{SeqInd}(n),\boxdot)

\displaystyle\mathsf{SeqTm}(t\;s)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}(\mathsf{SeqTm}(t),\leftVdash,\mathsf{SeqTm}(s),@)

\displaystyle\mathsf{SeqInd}(0)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}()

\displaystyle\mathsf{SeqTm}(\lambda.t)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}(\mathsf{SeqTm}(t),\lambda)

\displaystyle\mathsf{SeqInd}(n+1)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}(\mathsf{SeqInd}(n),\boxplus)

We write
C\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\mathsf{Seq}(t)$}}}C^{\prime}
for

C\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$F_{1}$}}}\ldots\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$F_{m}$}}}C^{\prime}
where

\mathsf{Seq}(t)=(F_{1},\ldots,F_{m})
.

Lemma 14
.

If
t^{\prime}
is closed, then

\langle 0,\odot,(t,e)\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\mathsf{Seq}(t^{\prime})$}}}\langle t,(t^{\prime},\epsilon)\mathop{::}e,[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}
.

Proof 5.1
.

Let
\eta=(t,e)
. First, for all
n
, we have

\langle 0,\rho,\eta\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}{\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\boxplus$}}}}^{n}\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\boxdot$}}}\langle n,n+1,\rho,\eta\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}
.
Then we show by induction on
t^{\prime}
that

\langle 0,\rho,\eta\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}\;\;\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\mathsf{SeqTm}(t^{\prime})$}}}\;\;\langle t^{\prime},\kappa,\rho,\eta\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}
where

\kappa=\max{(\mathsf{fv}(t^{\prime}))}+1
if
t^{\prime}
is not closed, and
\kappa=0
otherwise. The
case
t=n
is concluded with the previous observation. If

t^{\prime}=t^{\prime}_{1}\;t^{\prime}_{2}
, then

\displaystyle\langle 0,\rho,\eta\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}\;\;

\displaystyle\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\mathsf{SeqTm}(t^{\prime}_{1})$}}}\;\;\langle t^{\prime}_{1},\kappa_{1},\rho,\eta\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}\mbox{ (by induction)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\leftVdash$}}}\;\;\langle 0,(t^{\prime}_{1},\kappa_{1})\mathop{::}\rho,\eta\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\mathsf{SeqTm}(t^{\prime}_{2})$}}}\;\;\langle t^{\prime}_{2},\kappa_{2},(t^{\prime}_{1},\kappa_{1})\mathop{::}\rho,\eta\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}\mbox{ (by induction)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\;$}}}\;@\langle t^{\prime}_{1}\;t^{\prime}_{2},\max{(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2})},\rho,\eta\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}

By case analysis on
(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2})
, one can check that

\max{(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2})}
is the desired value. If
t^{\prime}=\lambda.t^{\prime\prime}
, then by
induction, we have

\langle 0,\rho,\eta\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}\;\;\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\mathsf{SeqTm}(t^{\prime\prime})$}}}\;\;\langle t^{\prime\prime},\kappa,\rho,\eta\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}
,
and then

\langle t^{\prime\prime},\kappa,\rho,\eta\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\lambda$}}}\langle t^{\prime},\kappa^{\prime},\rho,\eta\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}
where

\kappa^{\prime}
is as wished depending on
\kappa
.

This implies that for a closed term
t^{\prime}
, we have

\langle 0,\odot,\eta\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}\;\;\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\mathsf{SeqTm}(t^{\prime})$}}}\;\;\langle t^{\prime},0,\odot,\eta\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}
, and the
last transition
\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\medstar$}}}
gives what we want.

This lemma allows us to prove the correspondence between the AB machine and
applicative bisimilarity.

Theorem 15
.

(t,e)\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{app}}}(s,d)
iff
\langle t,e,[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{m}}}\langle s,d,[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}
.

Proof 5.2
.

To prove that machine bisimilarity implies applicative bisimilarity, we show
that

\mathord{\mathrel{\mathcal{R}}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}\{((t,e),(s,d))\;\;\mbox{\large{$\mid$}}\;\;\langle t,e,[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{m}}}\langle s,d,[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}\}

is an applicative bisimulation. Suppose

\langle t,e,[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}\mathrel{{\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\tau$}}}}^{*}}\langle\lambda.t^{\prime},e^{\prime},[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}
. Because
we have also

\langle\lambda.t^{\prime},e^{\prime},[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\ocirc$}}}\langle 0,\odot,(t^{\prime},e^{\prime})\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}
, there
exist
s^{\prime}
and
d^{\prime}
such that

\langle s,d,[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}\mathrel{{\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\tau$}}}}^{*}}\langle\lambda.s^{\prime},d^{\prime},[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\ocirc$}}}\langle 0,\odot,(s^{\prime},d^{\prime})\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}

and

\langle 0,\odot,(t^{\prime},e^{\prime})\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{m}}}\langle 0,\odot,(s^{\prime},d^{\prime})\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}
. Let
t^{\prime\prime}
be a closed term. By
Lemma
14
, we have

\langle 0,\odot,(t^{\prime},e^{\prime})\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\mathsf{Seq}(t^{\prime\prime})$}}}\langle t^{\prime},(t^{\prime\prime},\epsilon)\mathop{::}e^{\prime},[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}
, which can only be matched by

\langle 0,\odot,(s^{\prime},d^{\prime})\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\mathsf{Seq}(t^{\prime\prime})$}}}\langle s^{\prime},(t^{\prime\prime},\epsilon)\mathop{::}d^{\prime},[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}
. From

\langle 0,\odot,(t^{\prime},e^{\prime})\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{m}}}\langle 0,\odot,(s^{\prime},d^{\prime})\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}
, we get

\langle t^{\prime},(t^{\prime\prime},\epsilon)\mathop{::}e^{\prime},[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{m}}}\langle s^{\prime},(t^{\prime\prime},\epsilon)\mathop{::}d^{\prime},[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}
. It implies that

(t^{\prime},(t^{\prime\prime},\epsilon)\mathop{::}e^{\prime})\mathrel{\mathcal{R}}(s^{\prime},(t^{\prime\prime},\epsilon)\mathop{::}d^{\prime})
holds, as wished.

For the reverse implication, we show that

\displaystyle\mathord{\mathrel{\mathcal{R}}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}\{(\langle t,e,[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}},\langle s,d,[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}})\;\;\mbox{\large{$\mid$}}\;\;(t,e)\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{app}}}(s,d)\}

\displaystyle\cup\{(\langle n,\rho,(t,e)\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}),\langle n,\rho,(s,d)\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}})\;\;\mbox{\large{$\mid$}}\;\;(\lambda.t,e)\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{app}}}(\lambda.s,d)\}

\displaystyle\cup\{(\langle t^{\prime},\kappa,\rho,(t,e)\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}),\langle t^{\prime},\kappa,\rho,(s,d)\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}})\;\;\mbox{\large{$\mid$}}\;\;(\lambda.t,e)\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{app}}}(\lambda.s,d)\}

is a machine bisimulation. Suppose

\langle t,e,[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}\mathrel{\mathcal{R}}\langle s,d,[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}
. The only
possible flagged transition is

\langle t,e,[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}\mathrel{\mathrel{{\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\tau$}}}}^{*}}\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\ocirc$}}}}\langle 0,\odot,(t^{\prime},e^{\prime})\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}
, which implies in particular

\langle t,e,[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}\mathrel{{\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\tau$}}}}^{*}}\langle\lambda.t^{\prime},e^{\prime},[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}
.
Because
(t,e)\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{app}}}(s,d)
, there exists

(s^{\prime},d^{\prime})
such that

\langle s,d,[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}\mathrel{{\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\tau$}}}}^{*}}\langle\lambda.s^{\prime},d^{\prime},[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}
and

(\lambda.t^{\prime},e^{\prime})\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{app}}}(\lambda.s^{\prime},d^{\prime})
. We also have

\langle\lambda.s^{\prime},d^{\prime},[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\ocirc$}}}\langle 0,\odot,(s^{\prime},d^{\prime})\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}
, hence

\langle s,d,[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}\mathrel{\mathrel{{\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\tau$}}}}^{*}}\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\ocirc$}}}}\langle 0,\odot,(s^{\prime},d^{\prime})\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}
and

\langle 0,\odot,(t^{\prime},e^{\prime})\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}\mathrel{\mathcal{R}}\langle 0,\odot,(s^{\prime},d^{\prime})\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}

hold, as wished.

To cover the last two sets of the bisimulation, let

\langle t^{\prime},\kappa,\rho,(t,e)\rangle_{\mathsf{arg}}\mathrel{\mathcal{R}}\langle t^{\prime},\kappa,\rho,(s,d)\rangle_{\mathsf{arg}}
with
\mathsf{arg}\in\{\mathsf{ind},\mathsf{tm}\}
.
If
\langle t^{\prime},\kappa,\rho,(t,e)\rangle_{\mathsf{arg}}\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$F$}}}C
with

F\neq\medstar
, then

\langle t^{\prime},\kappa,\rho,(s,d)\rangle_{\mathsf{arg}}\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$F$}}}C^{\prime}
where
C^{\prime}

is the same as
C
except for the closures. These are not changed
by the transition and are still applicative bisimilar; we have
therefore
C\mathrel{\mathcal{R}}C^{\prime}
. Otherwise, we have

\langle t^{\prime},\kappa,\rho,(t,e)\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\medstar$}}}\langle t,(t^{\prime},\epsilon)\mathop{::}e,[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}
, and also

\langle t^{\prime},\kappa,\rho,(s,d)\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\medstar$}}}\langle s,(t^{\prime},\epsilon)\mathop{::}d,[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}
. The resulting configurations are in

\mathrel{\mathcal{R}}
because
(\lambda.t,e)\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{app}}}(\lambda.s,d)

implies

(t,(t^{\prime},\epsilon)\mathop{::}e)\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{app}}}(s,(t^{\prime},\epsilon)\mathop{::}d)

for all closed
t^{\prime}
.

5.3.
Translation into HOcore

We detail each component of the translation of the AB machine into
HOcore, starting with the evaluation mode, i.e., the KAM.

\displaystyle\left\ldbrack\langle t,e,\pi\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}\right\rdbrack

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}\left\ldbrack t\right\rdbrack\parallel\overline{env}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel\overline{c}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel P_{\mathsf{rec}}

\displaystyle\left\ldbrack(t,e)\right\rdbrack

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}\overline{\eta_{1}}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel\overline{\eta_{2}}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}

\displaystyle\left\ldbrack\eta\mathop{::}\pi\right\rdbrack

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}\overline{hd_{\mathsf{c}}}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel\overline{c}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}

\displaystyle\left\ldbrack\eta\mathop{::}e\right\rdbrack

\displaystyle\left\ldbrack[]\right\rdbrack

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}\ldots

\displaystyle\left\ldbrack\epsilon\right\rdbrack

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}{\boldsymbol{0}}

We follow the same principles as in Section
3
: a non-empty
stack
\pi
or environment
e
is represented by a pair of messages,
respectively on
hd_{\mathsf{c}}
and
c
, and
hd_{\mathsf{e}}
and
env
(we use the longer
env

for legibility reasons). A closure is
represented by two messages, one containing the term on
\eta_{1}
and
the other the environment on
\eta_{2}
. The process representing
the empty environment
\epsilon
should never be executed, because all the
closures we manipulate are closed; as a result, we can choose any
process to represent it, e.g.,
{\boldsymbol{0}}
. The empty stack
\left\ldbrack[]\right\rdbrack
and
the process
P_{\mathsf{rec}}
are used to generate an argument and are defined
and explained later.

\displaystyle\left\ldbrack t\;s\right\rdbrack

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}\mathsf{App_{ev}}(\left\ldbrack t\right\rdbrack,\left\ldbrack s\right\rdbrack)

\displaystyle\mathsf{App_{ev}}(P_{t},P_{s})

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}c(x).env(y).(P_{t}\parallel\overline{c}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel\overline{env}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom \displaystyle\left\ldbrack\lambda.t\right\rdbrack

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}\mathsf{Lam_{ev}}(\left\ldbrack t\right\rdbrack)

\displaystyle\mathsf{Lam_{ev}}(P_{t})

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}c(x).(x\parallel hd_{\mathsf{c}}(y).env(z).(P_{t}\parallel\overline{env}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}))

\displaystyle\left\ldbrack n+1\right\rdbrack

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}\mathsf{Ind_{ev}}(\left\ldbrack n\right\rdbrack)

\displaystyle\mathsf{Ind_{ev}}(P_{n})

\displaystyle\left\ldbrack 0\right\rdbrack

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}env(x).(x\parallel hd_{\mathsf{e}}(y).env(\_).(y\parallel\eta_{1}(y_{1}).\eta_{2}(y_{2}).(y_{1}\parallel\overline{env}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}})))

The encoding of
t\;s
simulates the rule
Push
: we receive the
current stack and environment
e
to create the new stack with
(s,e)
on
top. Because we receive the current environment to put it on the stack, we have
to recreate it on
env
, unchanged. In the encoding of
\lambda.t
, we capture
the stack and environment, and if the stack is non-empty, we fetch its head

\eta
to create a new environment with
\eta
on top. Finally, a de Bruijn
index
n>0
goes through the current environment, until we reach the correct
closure (case
n=0
). In that case, we receive the head
\eta
and tail of the
environment, we discard the tail as it is no longer useful, and we restore the term
and environment stored in
\eta
.

If
\lambda.t
is run in the environment
e
and the empty stack
[]
, then we
obtain

\left\ldbrack[]\right\rdbrack\parallel\mathsf{Stuck}((t,e))
,
where

\mathsf{Stuck}((t,e))\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}hd_{\mathsf{c}}(z).(\left\ldbrack t\right\rdbrack\parallel\overline{env}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}).

The process
\left\ldbrack[]\right\rdbrack
has to start the argument generating process, and the result has
then to be sent on
hd_{\mathsf{c}}
for the evaluation to restart. The process
\mathsf{Stuck}((t,e))
remains
stuck during the whole generation process. We now explain how
\langle n,\rho,\eta\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}
and
\langle t,\kappa,\rho,\eta\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}
are encoded, starting with
\kappa
and

\rho
.

\displaystyle\left\ldbrack\odot\right\rdbrack

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}mt(x).cs(\_).x

\displaystyle\left\ldbrack(t,\kappa)\right\rdbrack

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}\overline{w_{1}}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel\overline{w_{2}}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}

The machine distinguishes cases based on whether
\kappa
is
0
or not,
to know if we should apply the transition
Lambda
or

Lambda0
. In the encoding of these rules (see the definition
of
\mathsf{Lambda}
below), we send on name
zero
the expected behavior
if
\kappa=0
, and on
suc
what to do otherwise. The translation of the
counter receives both messages, executes the corresponding one (e.g.,
the one on
zero
for the encoding of
0
), and discards the
other. Apart from that,
\kappa
is translated as nested sending on name
suk
.
Similarly, the translation of
\rho
combines the regular
encodings of pairs and stacks, but also indicates whether
\rho
is
empty or not, to know if we can apply the transitions
App

and
Restart
.

\displaystyle\mathsf{Var}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}\boxdot(\_).ind(x).(\overline{tm}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel\overline{initTm}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}})

\displaystyle\left\ldbrack\langle t,\kappa,\rho,\eta\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}\right\rdbrack

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}\overline{tm}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel\overline{k}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel\overline{r}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel P_{\mathsf{rec}}\parallel\overline{initTm}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel\mathsf{Stuck}(\eta)

After flagging
\ocirc
, the process
\left\ldbrack[]\right\rdbrack
starts the argument generation
process in index mode: the index being built is sent on
ind
(here, initialized
with
\left\ldbrack 0\right\rdbrack
) and the stack on
r
. We also build on
k
the
counter
\kappa
corresponding to the index: at any point, we have
\kappa=n+1
. We keep two messages on
ind
and
k
encoding almost the same
information, but with different encodings, as the index
n
and the
counter
\kappa
are used differently.

The message on
initInd
triggers the recursive process
\mathsf{RecInd}
,
which non-deterministically chooses between
\mathsf{Succ}
and

\mathsf{Var}
. Executing
\mathsf{Succ}
flags
\boxplus
, increases the values of the
index (thanks to
\mathsf{Ind_{ev}}
) and the counter (with
\mathsf{Suk}
), and
relaunches the
\mathsf{RecInd}
process with a message on

initInd
. Executing
\mathsf{Var}
flags
\boxdot
, moves the index from
ind

to
tm
, and initiates the term mode by sending a message on
initTm
,
which triggers the recursive process
\mathsf{RecTm}
.

\displaystyle\mathsf{RecTm}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}initTm(\_).rectm(x).

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\left(\begin{aligned} x\parallel\overline{rectm}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel r(y).y&\parallel\overline{cs}\left\langle\begin{aligned} &k(z).hd_{\mathsf{r}}(y_{1}).r(y_{2}).\\
&(\mathsf{Lambda}(z)\parallel\overline{r}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}})+\mathsf{AppFun}(z,y)+\mathsf{App}(z,y_{1},y_{2})\end{aligned}\right\rangle\\
&\parallel\overline{mt}\left\langle k(z).\left(\begin{aligned} z&\parallel\overline{zero}\left\langle\begin{aligned} &\quad(\mathsf{Lambda}(z)\parallel\overline{r}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}})\\
&+\mathsf{AppFun}(z,\left\ldbrack\odot\right\rdbrack)+\mathsf{Done}\end{aligned}\right\rangle\\
&\parallel\overline{suc}\left\langle suk(\_).\begin{aligned} &\quad(\mathsf{Lambda}(z)\parallel\overline{r}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}})\\
&+\mathsf{AppFun}(z,\left\ldbrack\odot\right\rdbrack)\end{aligned}\right\rangle\end{aligned}\right)\right\rangle\end{aligned}\right)

The goal of
\mathsf{RecTm}
is to non-deterministically choose between the
four transitions available in term mode, namely
\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\lambda$}}}
,

\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\leftVdash$}}}
,
\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$@$}}}
, and
\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\medstar$}}}
. However, some of
these transitions have requirements:
\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$@$}}}
needs

\rho\neq\odot
and
\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\medstar$}}}
needs
\rho=\odot
and

\kappa=0
. The process
\mathsf{RecTm}
is therefore doing a case analysis to
check these conditions. First, it captures
\left\ldbrack\rho\right\rdbrack
on
r
: if

\rho\neq\odot
, it executes the message on
cs
, which makes a
choice between
\lambda
,
\leftVdash
, and
@
, which are represented
by respectively
\mathsf{Lambda}
,
\mathsf{AppFun}
, and
\mathsf{App}
. If

\rho=\odot
, then we do a case analysis on
\kappa
. If
\kappa=0
, then
we can do either
\lambda
,
\leftVdash
, or
\medstar
(represented by

\mathsf{Done}
), otherwise, only
\lambda
or
\leftVdash
are possible.

\displaystyle\mathsf{Lambda}(P_{\kappa})

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}\lambda(\_).tm(x).\left(\begin{aligned} \overline{tm}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel P_{\kappa}&\parallel\overline{zero}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\\
&\parallel\overline{suc}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\end{aligned}\right)

\displaystyle\mathsf{AppFun}(P_{\kappa},P_{\rho})

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}\leftVdash(\_).tm(x).\left(\begin{aligned} &\overline{r}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel\\
&\overline{ind}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel\overline{k}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel\overline{initInd}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}})\end{aligned}\right)

\displaystyle\mathsf{App}(P_{\kappa},P_{hd},P_{\rho})

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}@(\_).tm(x_{2}).

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\left(P_{hd}\parallel w_{2}(y).w_{1}(x_{1}).\left(\begin{aligned} &\overline{max1}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel\overline{max2}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel\overline{init1}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangl &resu(z).\left(\begin{aligned} &\overline{tm}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel\overline{r}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel\\
&\overline{k}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel\overline{initTm}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\end{aligned}\right)\end{aligned}\right)\right)

The process
\mathsf{Lambda}
adds a
\lambda
-abstraction to the term
in
tm
, updating
\kappa
(represented by
P_{\kappa}
) accordingly: if

\kappa=0
, then it is restored unchanged on
k
, otherwise, it is
decreased by 1 by releasing the message in
suk
. The process

\mathsf{AppFun}
pushes on the stack
P_{\rho}
the current term
t_{1}
on

tm
and its counter
\kappa_{1}
(represented by
P_{\kappa}
), which is the
term in function position of an application. It then relaunches the
index mode to build the argument
t_{2}
with its counter
\kappa_{2}
. The
process
\mathsf{App}
can then build the application itself, by computing
the maximum between
\kappa_{1}
and
\kappa_{2}
with the processes
\mathsf{RecMax}

and
\mathsf{Max}
.

\displaystyle\mathsf{RecMax}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}init1(x_{1}).init2(x_{2}).recmax(y).(y\parallel\overline{recmax}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel\mathsf{Max}(x_{1},x_{2}))

\displaystyle\mathsf{Max}(P_{1},P_{2})

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}P_{1}\begin{aligned} &\parallel\overline{zero}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\\
&\parallel\overline{suc}\left\langle suk(x_{1}).\left(\begin{aligned} P_{2}&\parallel\overline{zero}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\\
&\parallel\overline{suc}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\end{aligned}\right)\right\rangle\end{aligned}

We compute the maximum between
\kappa_{1}
and
\kappa_{2}
by removing the layers of
successors common to
\kappa_{1}
and
\kappa_{2}
, until we reach
0
for one of
them. If we reach
0
for
\kappa_{1}
first, then
\kappa_{2}
is the max, otherwise it
is
\kappa_{1}
. We store the initial values of
\kappa_{1}
and
\kappa_{2}
in
respectively
max1
and
max2
, and the decomposition occurs in
\mathsf{Max}
, where

init1
is initialized with
\kappa_{1}
and
init2
with
\kappa_{2}
. If

P_{1}=\left\ldbrack\kappa_{1}\right\rdbrack_{\mathsf{c}}=\left\ldbrack 0\right\rdbrack_{\mathsf{c}}
, then we send
\kappa_{2}
(stored in
max2
) on

resu
. Otherwise,
P_{1}=\overline{suk}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}
, and we do a case analysis on the
process
P_{2}=\left\ldbrack\kappa_{2}\right\rdbrack_{\mathsf{c}}
. If
P_{2}=\left\ldbrack 0\right\rdbrack_{\mathsf{c}}
, then we send
\kappa_{1}
on

resu
, otherwise
P_{2}=\overline{suk}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}
, and we restart
\mathsf{RecMax}
by
sending
P^{\prime}_{1}
and
P^{\prime}_{2}
on
init1
and
init2
, respectively. Once the max is
known on
resu
, then
\mathsf{App}
builds the application
t_{1}\;t_{2}
and
relaunches
\mathsf{RecTm}
.

\displaystyle\mathsf{Done}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}\medstar(\_).tm(x).(\overline{hd_{\mathsf{c}}}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel\overline{c}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}})

\displaystyle P_{\mathsf{rec}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}\mathsf{RecInd}\parallel\overline{recind}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel\mathsf{RecTm}\parallel\overline{rectm}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}\parallel\mathsf{RecMax}\parallel\overline{recmax}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}

Finally, the process
\mathsf{Done}
ends the argument generation phase, and restarts
the computation by restoring the empty continuation and by passing the term in

tm
to
\mathsf{Stuck}(\eta)
. The process
P_{\mathsf{rec}}
contains all the processes necessary
to encode the different recursive mechanisms.

Full abstraction

A case analysis on the configurations of
the machine shows that the translation of the AB machine satisfies the
conditions of Section
4.4
, and we can prove a full
abstraction result between AB machines and HOcore similar to
Theorem
12
. We deduce a full
abstraction result between the
\lambda
-calculus with applicative
bisimilarity and HOcore.

Corollary 16
.

For all closed closures
(t,e)
and
(s,d)
,

(t,e)\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{app}}}(s,d)
iff

\left\ldbrack\langle t,e,[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}\right\rdbrack\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{\scriptscriptstyle{HO}}}}\left\ldbrack\langle s,d,[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}\right\rdbrack
.

5.4.
Internalizing Contextual Equivalence

Corollary
16
is enough to deduce full abstraction
w.r.t. contextual equivalence, since

t\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{ctx}}}s\iff(t,\epsilon)\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{app}}}(s,\epsilon)
. However, it is
possible to prove a similar result directly, by internalizing
contextual equivalence in an abstract machine. We do so to show how
the resulting contextual equivalence machine differs from the AB
machine.

\displaystyle\langle n,\kappa,\rho\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\boxplus$}}}\langle n+1,\kappa+1,\rho\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}

(
Suc
)

\displaystyle\langle n,\kappa,\rho\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\boxdot$}}}\langle n,\kappa,\rho\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}

(
Var
)

\displaystyle\langle t,\kappa+1,\rho\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\lambda$}}}\langle\lambda.t,\kappa,\rho\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}

(
Lambda
)

\displaystyle\langle t,0,\rho\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\lambda$}}}\langle\lambda.t,0,\rho\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}

(
Lambda0
)

\displaystyle\langle t,\kappa,\rho\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\leftVdash$}}}\langle 0,1,(t,\kappa)\mathop{::}\rho\rangle_{\mathsf{ind}}

(
AppPush
)

\displaystyle\langle t,\kappa_{1},(s,\kappa_{2})\mathop{::}\rho\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\overleftarrow{@}$}}}\langle t\;s,\mathsf{max}(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2}),\rho\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}

(
\overleftarrow{\textsc{App}}
)

\displaystyle\langle t,\kappa_{1},(s,\kappa_{2})\mathop{::}\rho\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\overrightarrow{@}$}}}\langle s\;t,\mathsf{max}(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2}),\rho\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}

(
\overrightarrow{\textsc{App}}
)

\displaystyle\langle t,0,\odot\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\ostar$}}}\langle t,\epsilon,[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}

(
Start
)

\displaystyle\langle\lambda.t,e,[]\rangle_{\mathcal{}\mathsf{ev}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\medstar$}}}

(
Done
)

Figure 6.
Contextual equivalence machine

Figure
6
gives the transitions of the contextual
equivalence machine, except for the
\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\tau$}}}
transitions, which
are the same as in Section
5.1
. In contrast with the AB
machine, the contextual equivalence machine produces a context first,
and then reduces the resulting term; consequently, the starting point
is a state
\langle t,0,\odot\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}
, where
t
is the closed term we want to
plug in the context. When the context is finished, the transition

\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\ostar$}}}
switches to the evaluation mode. Also, the evaluation
part of the machine is not executed several times, since
\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{ctx}}}

is not coinductive. We flag
\medstar
when the evaluation terminates,
to distinguish a terminating term from a diverging one.

Creating a context
\mathcal{C}
is almost the same as generating an argument
in the AB machine, except that we want to plug a closed term
t

inside. We build
\mathcal{C}[t]
by starting the generation process
from
t
;
t
can be anywhere in
\mathcal{C}[t]
, not necessarily at
the leftmost position, so we cannot do the generation process going
left to right in an application, as with the AB machine
(Section
5.2
). Instead, after producing a term
t

and with a term
s
on the stack, we can do either the transition

AppLeft
to build
t\;s
, or
AppRight
to build

s\;t
.

{exa}

We show how to generate the context
\lambda.(0\;0)\;(\square\;0)

around
t
.

\displaystyle\langle t,0,\odot\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\leftVdash$}}}\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\boxdot$}}}\langle 0,1,(t,0)\mathop{::}\odot\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\overrightarrow{@}$}}}\langle t\;0,1,\odot\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\leftVdash$}}}\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\boxdot$}}}\langle 0,1,(t\;0,1)\mathop{::}\odot\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\leftVdash$}}}\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\boxdot$}}}\langle 0,1,(0,1)\mathop{::}(t\;0,1)\mathop{::}\odot\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\overrightarrow{@}$}}}\langle 0\;0,1,(t\;0,1)\mathop{::}\odot\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\overleftarrow{@}$}}}\langle(0\;0)\;(t\;0),1,\odot\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\xmapsto{\makebox[12.51709pt]{\scriptsize$\lambda$}}}\langle\lambda.(0\;0)\;(t\;0),0,\odot\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}

The translation of the contextual equivalence machine into HOcore and the full
abstraction proofs are similar to the AB machine ones.

Theorem 17
.

If
t
and
s
are closed terms, then
t\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{ctx}}}s
iff

\left\ldbrack\langle t,0,\odot\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}\right\rdbrack\mathrel{\approx_{\mathsf{\scriptscriptstyle{HO}}}}\left\ldbrack\langle s,0,\odot\rangle_{\mathsf{tm}}\right\rdbrack
.

6.
Call-by-Value

We adapt our techniques to the call-by-value setting, by internalizing
equivalences into the CK machine.

6.1.
The CK machine

As in call-by-name, we internalize
normal-form bisimilarity into a substitution-based abstract machine,
namely the CK machine
[
FFF09
]
.

\displaystyle C

\displaystyle\Coloneqq\langle t,\pi\rangle\;\;\mbox{\large{$\mid$}}\;\;\langle\pi,v\rangle_{\mathsf{ct}}

(configurations)

\displaystyle v

\displaystyle\Coloneqq x\;\;\mbox{\large{$\mid$}}\;\;\lambda x.t

(values)

\displaystyle t,s

\displaystyle\Coloneqq v\;\;\mbox{\large{$\mid$}}\;\;t\;s

(terms)

\displaystyle\pi

\displaystyle\Coloneqq\bullet\;t\mathop{::}\pi\;\;\mbox{\large{$\mid$}}\;\;v\;\bullet\mathop{::}\pi\;\;\mbox{\large{$\mid$}}\;\;[]

(stacks)

\displaystyle\langle t\;s,\pi\rangle

\displaystyle\mapsto\langle t,\bullet\;s\mathop{::}\pi\rangle

(
Fun
)

\displaystyle\langle v,\pi\rangle

\displaystyle\mapsto\langle\pi,v\rangle_{\mathsf{ct}}

(
Switch
)

\displaystyle\langle\bullet\;t\mathop{::}\pi,v\rangle_{\mathsf{ct}}

\displaystyle\mapsto\langle t,v\;\bullet\mathop{::}\pi\rangle

(
Arg
)

\displaystyle\langle\lambda x.t\;\bullet\mathop{::}\pi,v\rangle_{\mathsf{ct}}

\displaystyle\mapsto\langle[v\mathbin{/}x]t,\pi\rangle

(
Beta
)

The machine distinguishes configurations of the form
\langle t,\pi\rangle
,
which evaluates
t
in the stack
\pi
, from configurations

\langle\pi,v\rangle_{\mathsf{ct}}
, which decide how computation should proceed depending
on the stack
\pi
. In left-to-right call-by-value evaluation, we
evaluate the function before reducing its argument. What we remember
on the stack reflects this order:
\bullet\;t
means that we evaluate
the function and remembers its argument
t
, while
v\;\bullet
means
that we evaluate the argument of an already computed function
v
.

The
Fun
machine step focuses on the term in function
position. When it is evaluated, we look at the stack by switching to
the continuation mode (rule
Switch
). Either the top of the
stack is an argument which needs to be evaluated (rule
Arg
),
or it is a
\lambda
-abstraction ready to be applied to its argument
(rule
Beta
). When we reduce closed terms, we cannot get a
configuration of the form
\langle x\;\bullet\mathop{::}\pi,v\rangle_{\mathsf{ct}}
.

The encoding of the CK machine in HOcore is given in
Figure
7
. Unlike in the KAM, the stack has some
control on how the reduction proceeds. It is reflected in the encoding
of
\langle\pi,v\rangle_{\mathsf{ct}}
, where the encoded value
\left\ldbrack v\right\rdbrack_{\mathsf{v}}
is sent on a
channel name
val
, waiting to be consumed by the stack. The top of
the stack decides what becomes of
\left\ldbrack v\right\rdbrack_{\mathsf{v}}
: in the case of
\bullet\;t
,
the value
\left\ldbrack v\right\rdbrack_{\mathsf{v}}
is forwarded to the top of the stack and
\left\ldbrack t\right\rdbrack

can reduce. For
\lambda x.t\;\bullet
, the encoded
\lambda
-abstraction

val(x).\left\ldbrack t\right\rdbrack
is receiving
\left\ldbrack v\right\rdbrack_{\mathsf{v}}
, and the computation
continues as
[\left\ldbrack v\right\rdbrack_{\mathsf{v}}\mathbin{/}x]\left\ldbrack t\right\rdbrack
: as for the KAM, we encode

\beta
-reduction by a HOcore communication.

The encoding of values considered as terms
\left\ldbrack v\right\rdbrack
is uniform, and
recreates the encoding of
\langle\pi,v\rangle_{\mathsf{ct}}
by capturing the current
stack and running it in parallel with
\overline{val}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}
. The
encoding of the application
\left\ldbrack t\;s\right\rdbrack
is as expected: it runs

\left\ldbrack t\right\rdbrack
and pushes
\left\ldbrack s\right\rdbrack
on the current stack. Like for the KAM, the
encoding of the CK machine needs only two names,
c
and
val
.

We have a one-to-one correspondence between the CK-machine and its
translation, a result stronger than for the KAM
(cf. Theorem
1
): the administrative

Switch
step, which does not exist in the KAM, corresponds to
a communication in the HOcore translation.

Theorem 18
.

If
C\mapsto C^{\prime}
, then
\left\ldbrack C\right\rdbrack\mathrel{\xrightarrow{\makebox[54.40016pt]{\scriptsize$\tau$}}}\left\ldbrack C^{\prime}\right\rdbrack
. If
\left\ldbrack C\right\rdbrack\mathrel{\xrightarrow{\makebox[54.40016pt]{\scriptsize$\tau$}}}P
, then there exists
C^{\prime}
such that
P=\left\ldbrack C^{\prime}\right\rdbrack
.

\displaystyle\left\ldbrack\langle t,\pi\rangle\right\rdbrack

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}\left\ldbrack t\right\rdbrack\parallel\overline{c}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}

\displaystyle\left\ldbrack\langle\pi,v\rangle_{\mathsf{ct}}\right\rdbrack

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\smalltriangleup}}{{=}}\left\ldbrack\pi\right\rdbrack\parallel\overline{val}{{\mathchoice{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\textstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}{{}\mathopen{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\langle\vphantom{\cdot}\hskip-1.2pt\right.$}}}}\mathclose{{{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left.\hskip-1.2pt\vphantom{\cdot}\right\rangle$}}}}}}}

\displaystyle\left\ldbrack[]\right\rdbrack
